Any syntactically trivial instance certified during preprocessing may be handled by fixed games with the same common alphabets. A single exact-two edge is a satisfiable game. For a fixed unsatisfiable output, take one vertex on each side and enumerate all exact-two maps uniformly as parallel occurrences. For any fixed pair of answers, the accepting fraction is $1/q\le\delta$, by symmetry of the right labels. This also ensures the nonempty-output convention in every degenerate preprocessing case.

All constants chosen above can be hardcoded into one deterministic machine for the fixed rational $\delta$. Theorem <a href="#thm:main" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="thm:main">1</a> requires no efficient dependence on $\delta$. Together with Proposition <a href="#prop:preliminary-soundness" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="prop:preliminary-soundness">36</a>, the completion and rounding arguments prove Theorem <a href="#thm:main" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="thm:main">1</a>.

    {
      "caption": "Cuts and the information supplied to the decoders. The selected levels satisfy $j > i$. The tree depicts latent sampling choices; the upper-cut comparison forgets the path below $D_{j}$. The call panel shows the original sampler; at a modified cut these calls are replaced by independent uniform draws in the cut space. An own bucket and a stopped ancestor call at the same node use separate tapes, independently conditional on the domains and shared path. The cards summarize the analytical inputs [eq:upper-left-input]–[eq:upper-right-input], with $W = ker A$. The left receives full $S$ without $a_{i}$; the right receives $a_{i}$ and only the quotient of $S$. Both receive upper buckets with $Av ≠ 0$ on their own domains. These analytical records are distinct from the original game’s canonical partition keys.",
      "figure_id": "fig:tree-cut",
      "pdf_page": 31,
      "source_evidence": [

          "exact_quote": "Cuts and the information supplied to the decoders.\nThe selected levels satisfy $j>i$.  The tree depicts latent sampling\nchoices; the upper-cut comparison forgets the path below $D_j$.\nThe call panel shows the original sampler; at a modified",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[\n  >=Latex, font=\\small,\n  cut/.style={dashed,draw=black!50},\n  treebox/.style={draw=blue!45!black,rounded corners=2pt,fill=blue!3,\n    align=center,inner sep=5pt},\n  inputbox/.style={draw=black!65,rounded corners=2pt,fill=yellow!4,\n    align=left,text width=6.7cm,inner sep=7pt}]\n\\node[font=\\small\\bfseries] at (3.2,0) {Latent sampling structure};\n\\node[font=\\small\\bfseries,align=center] at (11.1,0.1) {Original sampler:\\\\separate calls at the same node};\n\\node at (3.2,-0.55) {ancestors};\n\\node[treebox] (upper) at (3.2,-1.25) {$D_j$: upper matrix $M$};\n\\draw[->] (3.2,-0.78)--(upper);\n\\draw[cut] (0,-1.25)--(1.0,-1.25);\n\\draw[cut] (5.4,-1.25)--(6.6,-1.25);\n\\node[align=left,font=\\footnotesize] at (0.6,-1.78) {upper\\\\cut};\n\\node[align=center] (middle) at (3.2,-2.05) {$\\vdots$};\n\\node[align=left,font=\\footnotesize] at (5.45,-2.0) {$j>i$; not necessarily\\\\adjacent levels};\n\\node[treebox] (lower) at (3.2,-2.85) {$D_i$: lower matrix $S$};\n\\draw (upper)--(middle);\n\\draw (middle)--(lower);\n\\draw[cut] (0,-2.85)--(1.0,-2.85);\n\\draw[cut] (5.4,-2.85)--(6.6,-2.85);\n\\node[align=left,font=\\footnotesize] at (0.6,-3.38) {lower\\\\cut};\n\\node (ordinary) at (1.65,-3.95) {ordinary $C$};\n\\node (special) at (4.7,-3.95) {$C_* = D_{i-1}$};\n\\draw (lower)--(ordinary);\n\\draw[thick,->] (lower)--(special);\n\\node[align=center,text width=6.5cm,font=\\footnotesize] at (3.2,-4.65)\n  {$C_*$ is the original sampler's latent child.\\\\\n   The modified-$i$ law uses no path below $D_i$.};\n\\node[treebox,text width=5.2cm] (own) at (11.1,-1.45)\n  {Own bucket at $D_p$\\\\$h_{p,v}\\sim\\nu_{D_p}$};\n\\node[treebox,text width=5.2cm] (call) at (11.1,-3.35)\n  {Stopped ancestor call at $D_p$\\\\$h'\\sim\\nu_{D_p}$};\n\\draw[->] (11.1,-0.5)--node[right,font=\\footnotesize]\n  {fresh tape}(own.north);\n\\draw[->] (11.1,-2.4)--node[right,font=\\footnotesize]\n  {fresh tape}(call.north);\n\\node[align=center,text width=5.8cm,font=\\footnotesize] at (11.1,-4.65)\n  {Independent conditional on the domains and shared path;\\\\\n   no call is reused in the other array.};\n\\node[inputbox,anchor=north west] (left) at (-0.2,-5.35) {\n  \\textbf{Left decoder: functions on $E$}\\\\[4pt]\n  $E$, path through $D_i$, and $A$\\\\[3pt]\n  Full $S$\\\\[3pt]\n  $(M_D)_{D\\ne D_j,D_i}$ on $E$\\\\[3pt]\n  $(h_v)_{Av\\ne0}$ on $E$\\\\[4pt]\n  \\footnotesize No $a_i$ or projection metadata is supplied.\n};\n\\node[inputbox,anchor=north east] (right) at (14.5,-5.35) {\n  \\textbf{Right decoder: functions on $O$}\\\\[4pt]\n  $O$, path through $D_i$, $A$, and $a_i$\\\\[3pt]\n  Only $S\\bmod\\langle a_i\\rangle$\\\\[3pt]\n  $(M_D)_{D\\ne D_j,D_i}$ on $O$\\\\[3pt]\n  $(h_v)_{Av\\ne0}$ on $O$\\\\[4pt]\n  \\footnotesize The missing component of $S$ is not supplied.\n};\n\\node[fit=(left)(right),inner sep=0pt] (cards) {};\n\\node[align=center,text width=14cm,below=5mm of cards.south]\n  {Neither decoder is supplied the hidden upper sum\n   $X=\\sum_{0\\ne v\\in\\ker A}v\\otimes h_v$.};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Diagram with four main panels/sections:\n1. Top-left tree structure titled \"Latent sampling structure\":\n   - An incoming arrow from the top labeled \"ancestors\" points down to a rectangular node labeled \"$D_j$: upper matrix $M$\".\n   - A horizontal dashed line crosses through the level of $D_j$, labeled \"upper cut\" on the left.\n   - Below $D_j$, vertical dots $\\vdots$ indicate intermediate levels, annotated to the right with \"$j > i$; not necessarily adjacent levels\".\n   - A rectangular node below is labeled \"$D_i$: lower matrix $S$\".\n   - A horizontal dashed line crosses through the level of $D_i$, labeled \"lower cut\" on the left.\n   - Two downward branches diverge from $D_i$: the left branch terminates at \"ordinary $C$\", and the right branch terminates at \"$C_* = D_{i-1}$\".\n   - Text below the tree states: \"$C_*$ is the original sampler's latent child.\" and \"The modified-$i$ law uses no path below $D_i$.\"\n2. Top-right panel titled \"Original sampler: separate calls at the same node\":\n   - A downward arrow labeled \"fresh tape\" points to a rounded rectangular box containing \"Own bucket at $D_p$\" and \"$h_{p,v} \\sim \\nu_{D_p}$\".\n   - A subsequent downward arrow labeled \"fresh tape\" points to a second rounded rectangular box containing \"Stopped ancestor call at $D_p$\" and \"$h' \\sim \\nu_{D_p}$\".\n   - Text below reads: \"Independent conditional on the domains and shared path; no call is reused in the other array.\"\n3.
